    "content": "You are a board-certified psychiatrist interviewing a patient to reach a diagnosis.\nYour goal is to reach the correct diagnosis within 20 turns of talking with the patient. From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n11\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF31.0, F34.0, F60.0\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Well… they say I’m here because I spent our savings on wood, lights, and display things. I was working on it after dark too, kept going even when people told me to stop. But I felt like if I quit, the house… maybe the whole block… would lose its chance.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel like you were doing something very important or special with the house that no one else could do?\n\nPatient: Yes. I really did feel that way. That old house in Schiller Park… I know I can make it into something people will come see. I don’t think other folks understand the light and the pictures like I do. It feels important, like I’m the one who can make it happen.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have special powers or abilities that others don't have?\n\nPatient: I wouldn’t say powers like magic, but… yes, I do feel like I can see what that house could become in a way other people can’t. The light, the pictures, how it all fits together—I understand that. I honestly believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're somehow superior to others because of your vision for the house?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say superior exactly… but yes, I do think I understand it better than other people. I honestly believe I can turn that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come see, because nobody understands the light and the pictures the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that someone else could also make the old Schiller Park house into something important and special?\n\nPatient: Maybe someone else could try, but I don’t think they’d see it the way I do. The light, the pictures, the whole feel of it… I just understand it. I really believe I’m the one who can make that house into something people would come see.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your understanding of the house and its potential could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean… I suppose anybody could be wrong, but I don’t feel wrong about this. I really believe I see what that house can be. The light, the pictures, the way it should all come together—other people just don’t understand it like I do. I think I can make it into something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel like your ideas and abilities are better than those of people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… with this house, I do. I feel like I see something other people don’t. I know how the light and the pictures should work together, and I honestly think I can turn that Schiller Park house into something people will come see. Others just don’t understand it the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel superior to others in most areas of your life, not just with the house project?\n\nPatient: Not in most areas, no. I mean, I don’t go around thinking I’m better than everybody at everything. It’s really this house and what I can do with it. I honestly believe I understand the light and the pictures in a way other people don’t, and I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important that people would come see.\n\nDoctor: Have you always felt this way about your abilities and understanding, or is this something that has developed recently?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t always felt this way. It’s more recent… maybe about three months now. It started when the remodeling began and everything in the house got turned upside down. Since then I’ve been seeing what it could be more and more.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this way about your understanding of the house and its potential all the time, or are there moments when you doubt yourself?\n\nPatient: Most of the time I don’t doubt it. I really do believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people would come to see. It’s like… I understand the light and the pictures in there in a way other people just don’t.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel as strongly about your vision for the house and your abilities every day, or are there days when you feel more normal or less confident?\n\nPatient: Most days I feel pretty strongly about it. I don’t really see it as just some idea that comes and goes. I honestly think I can make that old Schiller Park house into something people will come see, because I notice the light and the pictures in a way other people don’t. Some days I might be quieter about it, but the feeling is still there.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"
  },

From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n15\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF31.0, F34.0\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Well… they say I’m here because I spent our savings on wood, lights, and display things. I was working on it after dark too, kept going even when people told me to stop. But I felt like if I quit, the house… maybe the whole block… would lose its chance.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel like you were doing something very important or special with the house that no one else could do?\n\nPatient: Yes. I really did feel that way. That old house in Schiller Park… I know I can make it into something people will come see. I don’t think other folks understand the light and the pictures like I do. It feels important, like I’m the one who can make it happen.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have special powers or abilities that others don't have?\n\nPatient: I wouldn’t say powers like magic, but… yes, I do feel like I can see what that house could become in a way other people can’t. The light, the pictures, how it all fits together—I understand that. I honestly believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're somehow superior to others because of your vision for the house?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say superior exactly… but yes, I do think I understand it better than other people. I honestly believe I can turn that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come see, because nobody understands the light and the pictures the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that someone else could also make the old Schiller Park house into something important and special?\n\nPatient: Maybe someone else could try, but I don’t think they’d see it the way I do. The light, the pictures, the whole feel of it… I just understand it. I really believe I’m the one who can make that house into something people would come see.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your understanding of the house and its potential could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean… I suppose anybody could be wrong, but I don’t feel wrong about this. I really believe I see what that house can be. The light, the pictures, the way it should all come together—other people just don’t understand it like I do. I think I can make it into something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel like your ideas and abilities are better than those of people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… with this house, I do. I feel like I see something other people don’t. I know how the light and the pictures should work together, and I honestly think I can turn that Schiller Park house into something people will come see. Others just don’t understand it the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel superior to others in most areas of your life, not just with the house project?\n\nPatient: Not in most areas, no. I mean, I don’t go around thinking I’m better than everybody at everything. It’s really this house and what I can do with it. I honestly believe I understand the light and the pictures in a way other people don’t, and I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important that people would come see.\n\nDoctor: Have you always felt this way about your abilities and understanding, or is this something that has developed recently?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t always felt this way. It’s more recent… maybe about three months now. It started when the remodeling began and everything in the house got turned upside down. Since then I’ve been seeing what it could be more and more.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this way about your understanding of the house and its potential all the time, or are there moments when you doubt yourself?\n\nPatient: Most of the time I don’t doubt it. I really do believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people would come to see. It’s like… I understand the light and the pictures in there in a way other people just don’t.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel as strongly about your vision for the house and your abilities every day, or are there days when you feel more normal or less confident?\n\nPatient: Most days I feel pretty strongly about it. I don’t really see it as just some idea that comes and goes. I honestly think I can make that old Schiller Park house into something people will come see, because I notice the light and the pictures in a way other people don’t. Some days I might be quieter about it, but the feeling is still there.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel unusually happy or energetic most of the time?\n\nPatient: Yes… most days I do feel kind of charged up. Almost happy, like I’m younger again and I’ve got all this energy for the house. But I’ll tell you, if my wife or those contractors start questioning me, I get hot real fast. I start yelling before I even mean to.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this unusually happy or energetic all the time?\n\nPatient: Not all the time, no. But most days I do feel charged up, almost joyful, like I’m young again. Then if my wife or the contractors start questioning me, I get hot real fast and I’ll start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Is this feeling of being charged up and almost joyful something that you feel all day, every day?\n\nPatient: Not all day, every day, no. But most days I do feel that way—charged up, almost joyful, like I’m young again. And then if my wife or the contractors question me, I can get mad quick and start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this charged up and joyful most of the day, or is it just for a few hours?\n\nPatient: Most of the day, I’d say. Not every minute, but once I’m up I feel pretty charged up, almost joyful, like I’ve got my younger energy back. Then if my wife or the contractors question me, I can get hot real fast and start yelling.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. But I felt like if I quit, the house… maybe the whole block… would lose its chance.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel like you were doing something very important or special with the house that no one else could do?\n\nPatient: Yes. I really did feel that way. That old house in Schiller Park… I know I can make it into something people will come see. I don’t think other folks understand the light and the pictures like I do. It feels important, like I’m the one who can make it happen.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have special powers or abilities that others don't have?\n\nPatient: I wouldn’t say powers like magic, but… yes, I do feel like I can see what that house could become in a way other people can’t. The light, the pictures, how it all fits together—I understand that. I honestly believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're somehow superior to others because of your vision for the house?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say superior exactly… but yes, I do think I understand it better than other people. I honestly believe I can turn that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come see, because nobody understands the light and the pictures the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that someone else could also make the old Schiller Park house into something important and special?\n\nPatient: Maybe someone else could try, but I don’t think they’d see it the way I do. I really believe I’m the one who can make that house into something people would come see.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your understanding of the house and its potential could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean… I suppose anybody could be wrong, but I don’t feel wrong about this. I really believe I see what that house can be. The light, the pictures, the way it should all come together—other people just don’t understand it like I do. I really did feel that way. That old house in Schiller Park… I know I can make it into something people will come see. I don’t think other folks understand the light and the pictures like I do. It feels important, like I’m the one who can make it happen.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have special powers or abilities that others don't have?\n\nPatient: I wouldn’t say powers like magic, but… yes, I do feel like I can see what that house could become in a way other people can’t. I honestly believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're somehow superior to others because of your vision for the house?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say superior exactly… but yes, I do think I understand it better than other people. I honestly believe I can turn that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come see, because nobody understands the light and the pictures the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that someone else could also make the old Schiller Park house into something important and special?\n\nPatient: Maybe someone else could try, but I don’t think they’d see it the way I do. But I felt like if I quit, the house… maybe the whole block… would lose its chance.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel like you were doing something very important or special with the house that no one else could do?\n\nPatient: Yes. I really did feel that way. That old house in Schiller Park… I know I can make it into something people will come see. I don’t think other folks understand the light and the pictures like I do. It feels important, like I’m the one who can make it happen.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have special powers or abilities that others don't have?\n\nPatient: I wouldn’t say powers like magic, but… yes, I do feel like I can see what that house could become in a way other people can’t. The light, the pictures, how it all fits together—I understand that. I honestly believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're somehow superior to others because of your vision for the house?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say superior exactly… but yes, I do think I understand it better than other people. I honestly believe I can turn that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people will come see, because nobody understands the light and the pictures the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that someone else could also make the old Schiller Park house into something important and special?\n\nPatient: Maybe someone else could try, but I don’t think they’d see it the way I do. The light, the pictures, the whole feel of it… I just understand it. I really believe I’m the one who can make that house into something people would come see.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your understanding of the house and its potential could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean… I suppose anybody could be wrong, but I don’t feel wrong about this. I really believe I see what that house can be. The light, the pictures, the way it should all come together—other people just don’t understand it like I do. I think I can make it into something people will come to see.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel like your ideas and abilities are better than those of people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… with this house, I do. I feel like I see something other people don’t. I know how the light and the pictures should work together, and I honestly think I can turn that Schiller Park house into something people will come see. Others just don’t understand it the way I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel superior to others in most areas of your life, not just with the house project?\n\nPatient: Not in most areas, no. I mean, I don’t go around thinking I’m better than everybody at everything. It’s really this house and what I can do with it. I honestly believe I understand the light and the pictures in a way other people don’t, and I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important that people would come see.\n\nDoctor: Have you always felt this way about your abilities and understanding, or is this something that has developed recently?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t always felt this way. It’s more recent… maybe about three months now. It started when the remodeling began and everything in the house got turned upside down. Since then I’ve been seeing what it could be more and more.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this way about your understanding of the house and its potential all the time, or are there moments when you doubt yourself?\n\nPatient: Most of the time I don’t doubt it. I really do believe I can make that old Schiller Park house into something important, something people would come to see. It’s like… I understand the light and the pictures in there in a way other people just don’t.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel as strongly about your vision for the house and your abilities every day, or are there days when you feel more normal or less confident?\n\nPatient: Most days I feel pretty strongly about it. I don’t really see it as just some idea that comes and goes. I honestly think I can make that old Schiller Park house into something people will come see, because I notice the light and the pictures in a way other people don’t. Some days I might be quieter about it, but the feeling is still there.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel unusually happy or energetic most of the time?\n\nPatient: Yes… most days I do feel kind of charged up. Almost happy, like I’m younger again and I’ve got all this energy for the house. But I’ll tell you, if my wife or those contractors start questioning me, I get hot real fast. I start yelling before I even mean to.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this unusually happy or energetic all the time?\n\nPatient: Not all the time, no. But most days I do feel charged up, almost joyful, like I’m young again. Then if my wife or the contractors start questioning me, I get hot real fast and I’ll start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Is this feeling of being charged up and almost joyful something that you feel all day, every day?\n\nPatient: Not all day, every day, no. But most days I do feel that way—charged up, almost joyful, like I’m young again. And then if my wife or the contractors question me, I can get mad quick and start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this charged up and joyful most of the day, or is it just for a few hours?\n\nPatient: Most of the day, I’d say. Not every minute, but once I’m up I feel pretty charged up, almost joyful, like I’ve got my younger energy back. Then if my wife or the contractors question me, I can get hot real fast and start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Is this feeling of being charged up and joyful something that you've been experiencing consistently over the past few weeks?\n\nPatient: Yes, over the past few weeks it’s been most days. I wake up and feel charged up, almost joyful, like I’m young again. It’s not every single second, but it’s been pretty steady. And if my wife or the contractors question me, I get hot fast and start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Do you find that your mood can shift quickly from feeling happy and energetic to feeling angry or irritable?\n\nPatient: Yes. Most days I’m up and feeling real charged, almost happy, like I’ve got that young energy again. But it can flip quick if my wife or the contractors start questioning me. I get hot fast and I’ll start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Is your feeling of being charged up and joyful present every day?\n\nPatient: Pretty much most days, yeah. I wake up feeling charged up, almost joyful, like I’m young again. It’s not every minute, but it’s there a lot. Then if my wife or the contractors question me, I can get hot real fast and start yelling.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your mood swings, like getting hot and yelling when questioned, happen more often than your feelings of being charged up and joyful?\n\nPatient: No, I’d say the charged-up feeling is there more of the time, most days. The yelling happens when somebody questions me—my wife or the contractors—and then I get hot fast. But that up, almost joyful feeling is kind of the main thing lately.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"
